# MASTER PAPER INDEX — GSE research corpus

Every arXiv paper in the GSE research corpus, indexed once. Generated by reading module
headers (first ~30 lines) and the corpus documentation. Nothing here was executed; no
test was run; no result is claimed.

## How this was enumerated (observed, not assumed)

- **Code discovery.** `packages/prediction-engine/src/**/*.ts` excluding `*.test.ts` and
  matching `^(\d{4})-(\d{4,5})(v\d+)?-.+\.ts$` → **308 files, 308 unique arXiv IDs, zero
  duplicate IDs** (checked by grouping on the normalized dotted id). 181 sit in family
  directories; **127 sit at the engine root** (`src/NNNN-NNNNN*.ts`).
  - The task brief expected "~254" and said to exclude the engine root. Both numbers are
    wrong for this worktree: the family-scoped glob yields **181**, and the engine root
    holds **127** further numbered arXiv modules, not engine wiring. Total numbered
    modules = **308**. Part A covers the family-scoped set as instructed; **Appendix A**
    lists the 127 engine-root modules so nothing is silently dropped.
- **Documentation cross-reference (both directions).** `docs/research/2026-09-21/arxiv-deep/`
  holds 1,511 ledgers. 1,485 resolve to a primary arXiv ID from their `#` heading (or, failing
  that, from an `arXiv:` citation in their first 12 lines); **26 do not** and are listed as a gap
  at the end of Part B. Those 1,485 ledgers carry **1,467 unique primary IDs**.
  - Doc side → code side: **1,186 ledgers have no code module** (Part B: 1,174 `no file`,
    12 `duplicate`); the other 299 primaries do have one.
  - Code side → doc side: a looser scan of the first 30 lines of every ledger (not just the
    heading) matches **306 of the 308** code IDs. **Exactly one code module has no arxiv-deep
    ledger**: `1602.00542` → `experimental/1602-00542-cluster-seeking-js.ts`. The 27-module
    gap between 306 and 308 is headings-vs-body: those papers are cited inside another
    ledger's text rather than being that ledger's primary subject.
- **Scans beyond arxiv-deep.** `docs/research` as a whole (12 dated subtrees, 2,100+ files)
  contains no arXiv ID that is absent from arxiv-deep, so nothing is missed by scoping the
  doc side to arxiv-deep.
- **The worktree is live.** Other agents were writing to it while this index was built, so the
  family-module count moved 179 → 180 → 181 during the run. The final reverse check — every
  on-disk family module ID present in Part A, and every Part A ID present on disk — passed at
  **181/181 with 0 missing and 0 extra**. That is the number to trust. Re-run the glob before
  relying on any total here.

## Column and vocabulary rules

- **arXiv id** — `NNNN.NNNNN` taken from the filename. The version suffix is preserved
  in the same cell as `(vN)` so the id itself stays clean and no sixth column is added.
- **title** — verbatim from the module's JSDoc first line. Where the header carries no
  paper title, the literal `TITLE NOT IN HEADER` is written. Mojibake in the source
  (`â€”`, `Î±`, `âˆ’`) was decoded to the intended character; nothing else was reworded.
- **task area** — exactly one of `JS / SUTVA / tracking / forecasting / DFS / other`,
  chosen by **what the module's ACCEPTANCE GATE tests**:
  - `JS` — the gate tests joint scoring: coherent win + margin/total, score distributions,
    Skellam, bivariate/Conway–Maxwell–Poisson, expected points.
  - `SUTVA` — the gate tests a causal estimand: treatment effect, interference, matching,
    IPW/DR, DAG discovery, counterfactual.
  - `tracking` — the gate tests a trajectory, pose, motion or video representation.
  - `forecasting` — the gate tests a forward prediction: next event, next game, in-play
    state, drift, season trajectory, holdout log-loss on a future quantity.
  - `DFS` — the gate tests a fantasy/lineup/roster/slate/stack quantity.
  - `other` — the gate tests a decision rule, a structural property, or a mechanism that
    is not a forward prediction (sizing, abstention thresholds, ensemble weights,
    calibration, symbolic-equation recovery, NLP).
- **status** — `has file` / `no file` / `duplicate` / `off-domain`.
  `duplicate` requires two indexed files sharing an arXiv ID: **there are none.**
  `off-domain` is used only where the module is clearly not sports-predictive.
- **one-line summary** — a single factual sentence taken from the module header. No
  results, thresholds or performance numbers are asserted beyond what the header states.

# APPENDIX A — Numbered modules at the engine root (excluded from Part A per brief)

Scope: `packages/prediction-engine/src/NNNN-NNNNN*.ts` (top level of the engine root), `*.test.ts`
excluded. **127 rows**, all with unique arXiv IDs. The brief said to exclude the engine root, so
these are held here rather than merged into the Part A counts. They are numbered arXiv research
modules of the same kind as Part A, not engine wiring, and dropping them would lose a third of
the corpus. Status: `has file` 122, `off-domain` 5, `duplicate` 0.

`off-domain` is applied only where the paper's own subject is clearly not sports-predictive
(gaming spend propensity, recommender-system bandits, quantitative trading, political polling,
transit passenger flow). In each case the module is a method transplant into a sports lane, not
a sports model of that subject.

# PART B — Papers documented in the corpus with NO code file

Source: `docs/research/2026-09-21/arxiv-deep/` (1,511 ledgers). Each ledger's primary arXiv ID is
taken from its `#` heading; the title is that heading verbatim; the one-line summary is the ledger's
`**Verdict:**` line, which is the single factual sentence the ledger header states. Where a ledger has
no `**Verdict:**` line in its first 12 lines, the literal `VERDICT LINE NOT IN HEADER` is written
rather than a substitute.

**1,186 rows.** Status: `no file` 1,174, `duplicate` 12 (two arxiv-deep ledgers whose primary heading
resolves to the same arXiv ID; the first occurrence is kept, the later one is marked `duplicate`).
26 ledgers could not be resolved to a primary arXiv ID from their heading or first 12 lines and are
**not** listed — they are recorded in the gap note at the end of this part.

**Task area for this part is mechanical, not hand-judged.** The rule is a documented keyword pass
over title + verdict text, applied in order: SUTVA (`causal`, `treatment effect`, `counterfactual`,
`interference`, `spillover`, `doubly robust`, `IPTW`, `instrumental variable`, `difference-in-difference`,
`propensity`, `matching`, `confound`, `mediation`, `synthetic control`, `structural equation`) →
JS (`expected point`, `joint score`, `skellam`, `bivariate poisson`, `Conway`, `Maxwell`,
`zero-inflated`, `score distribution`, `margin distribution`, `point distribution`) → tracking
(`tracking data`, `trajector`, `pose estim`, `multi-object tracking`, `motion capture`, `optical flow`,
`human pose`, `skeleton`, `action recognition`, `video`, `spatial analytics`) → DFS (`daily fantasy`,
`DFS`, `fantasy premier`, `fantasy basketball`, `lineup optimi`, `salary cap`, `roster optimi`,
`auction`, `best ball`, `survivor`, `fantasy points`) → forecasting (`forecast`, `nowcast`,
`state space`, `time series`, `survival analys`, `changepoint`, `hazard`, `predict`, `drift`) →
other. Ledger authors do not write acceptance gates, so the gate-based rule that governs Part A
cannot apply here. Treat this column as a keyword label, not a reading of the paper.

| arXiv id | title | task area | status | one-line summary |

# SUMMARY

**1,494 rows total** across three tables.

| table | rows | scope |
|---|---|---|
| Part A | 181 | family-directory modules, `packages/prediction-engine/src/<family>/` |
| Appendix A | 127 | engine-root modules `packages/prediction-engine/src/NNNN-NNNNN*.ts` |
| Part B | 1,186 | papers documented in `arxiv-deep/` with no code file |

## By status (all 1,494 rows)

| status | count | meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `has file` | 303 | a numbered module implements the paper (Part A 181 + Appendix A 122) |
| `no file` | 1,174 | documented in `arxiv-deep/`, no module exists |
| `duplicate` | 12 | two arxiv-deep ledgers whose primary heading resolves to the same arXiv ID |
| `off-domain` | 5 | paper's own subject is clearly not sports-predictive (Appendix A) |

**`duplicate` for code modules: zero.** All 308 numbered modules carry distinct arXiv IDs.

## By task area (all 1,494 rows)

| task area | Part A | Appendix A | Part B | total |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| JS | 22 | 21 | 11 | 54 |
| SUTVA | 11 | 9 | 60 | 80 |
| tracking | 6 | 15 | 154 | 175 |
| forecasting | 40 | 25 | 368 | 433 |
| DFS | 15 | 13 | 20 | 48 |
| other | 87 | 44 | 573 | 704 |
| **total** | **181** | **127** | **1,186** | **1,494** |

Part A and Appendix A task areas were read off each module's ACCEPTANCE GATE. Part B task areas
are a mechanical keyword label (rule stated above that table) because ledgers carry verdicts, not
gates.

## Known gaps, stated rather than filled

1. **26 arxiv-deep ledgers resolve to no arXiv ID** from their heading or first 12 lines. They are
   not listed. Most are `Ledger NNNN` and `Deep-Read Ledger NNNN` documents whose arXiv ID lives
   further down, plus range-form citations such as `(arXiv:2209.07581–2209.08778)` which encode
   two papers in one heading. Each of those papers is either already in Part A (if a module
   exists) or still unindexed.
2. **1,174 papers are documented but not implemented.** That is the largest number in this index
   and it is the point of the cross-reference: the corpus knows far more than the engine has
   built. It is a coverage measurement, not a quality claim about any of them.
3. **`experimental/1902-07378-gp-prior-paired-comparison.ts` is not evidence for its paper.** Its
   own header says it was written from the title alone with no access to the paper text, and
   directs that it not be described as a reproduction. This index records that claim rather than
   softening it.
4. **No module was executed and no test was run.** Every summary sentence is a transcription of a
   header, not a result. No threshold, log-loss figure or acceptance outcome in any header is
   asserted here as observed behaviour.
5. **Counts drift.** This worktree was being written to during the run (family modules 179 → 181).
   Re-run the enumeration before treating any total as current.
